```text
Based on the search results, I can provide you with comprehensive information about the ACL Rolling Review 2026 May cycle and EMNLP 2026 deadlines:

## ACL Rolling Review 2026 May Cycle Deadlines

The ARR submission deadline for long and short papers is May 25, 2026, with a reviewer registration deadline for ALL authors of May 27, 2026.

## Key May Cycle Dates

Author response and author-reviewer discussion runs July 7 - July 13, 2026, with meta reviews released July 30, 2026.

## EMNLP 2026 Commitment Deadline

The EMNLP commitment deadline is August 2, 2026.

## Important Policy for EMNLP Submissions

Given that the ARR 2026 May cycle is aligned with two conferences (EMNLP and AACL), authors will need to explicitly declare which conference they intend to commit to at submission time. This choice will be binding for EMNLP 2026: i.e., ARR 2026 May submissions that do not select EMNLP 2026 during submission will not be able to commit to EMNLP 2026. However, this policy does NOT apply to any papers submitted and reviewed in prior ARR cycles that wish to commit to EMNLP.
```

```text
{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"experiments\": [{\"id\": \"plan_001\", \"name\": \"real_data_dif_mmlu\", \"claim\": 1, \"description\": \"Apply DIF analysis to METABENCH MMLU response matrix (5,219 models × 12,508 items). Data characteristics: only 37% models classifiable by family (1,929); effective temporal span ~1.5 years (2023: N=1,253, 2024-H1: N=656); 15.4% items have negative discrimination. Design: (a) Primary cohort: temporal split (2023 vs 2024-H1, both N≥80 ✓) (b) Alternative cohort: accuracy-quintile split (ability-based grouping, uses all models) (c) Item filtering: run with and without negative-discrimination items (sensitivity analysis) Run ETS C DIF detection on all items under both cohort definitions. Proxy validation against Li et al. (EMNLP 2024) web-overlap contamination labels (9,229 items, 73.8% coverage). IMPORTANT conceptual distinction: Li et al. labels are model-agnostic (item exists on web), while DIF detects cohort-differential contamination (item behaves differently across temporal cohorts). Low concordance is EXPECTED — DIF only catches items where contamination is differential across cohorts, not uniform. Primary validation metric: FPR analysis (DIF flags on Li-et-al-clean items) rather than TPR (which conflates uniform vs differential contamination). Unlabeled items (26.2%) reported separately as \\\"unknown\\\". plan_011 (semi-synthetic injection) provides controlled ground truth to complement this proxy. (d) Within-family confound control: run DIF within model families where both versions\\n    have N≥80. Confirmed feasible: Llama-2 (N=161) vs Llama-3 (N=308) ✓. Qwen-1.5 (N=67)\\n    below threshold — use as supplementary only. Identify additional qualifying pairs from\\n    METABENCH metadata. If DIF flags\\n    items that are NOT differentially contaminated within-family, the flag reflects capability\\n    gain, not contamination. Report: DIF flag overlap between cross-cohort and within-family\\n    analyses to disentangle contamination from capability improvement.\\n(e) Domain-specificity analysis: correlate DIF flags with MMLU subject categories to check\\n    whether flags cluster in domains with known capability jumps vs known contamination.\\nReport: DIF flags × cohort definition × item filtering — 4-way sensitivity matrix + within-family confound control + domain-specificity analysis.\\n\", \"priority\": \"P0\", \"gpu_budget_hours\": 0, \"deps\": [], \"registry_id\": \"\", \"status\": \"planned\"}, {\"id\": \"plan_002\", \"name\": \"rl_robustness_validation\", \"claim\": 1, \"description\": \"Empirically validate DIF's RL-robustness claim. CRITICAL DISTINCTION: - \\\"Alignment RL\\\" (DPO/ORPO/KTO) = offline preference optimization, does NOT produce\\n  the NLL gap contraction that Wang et al. demonstrate. METABENCH has 477 such models.\\n- \\\"Reasoning RL\\\" (GRPO/PPO online training) = the mechanism that conceals contamination\\n  signals. This is what Claim 1 targets. METABENCH has almost none of these.\\nDesign (Reviewer-validated): (a) SFT-only cohort from METABENCH as baseline (b) Alignment-RL cohort (DPO/ORPO, N=477) from METABENCH as mechanism control —\\n    DPO doesn't use PPO clipping, so no NLL gap contraction; if DIF works on both\\n    (a) and (b), robustness covers the full RL spectrum\\n(c) Reasoning-RL models (DeepSeek-R1-7B/32B, Qwen2.5-RL, etc.) — run MMLU inference\\n    on 10-15 open-weight GRPO/PPO models. These are ADDED to the focal cohort\\n    (not a standalone group): DIF is group-level, so we verify that adding RL models\\n    to a cohort doesn't degrade detection. Separately, run traditional detectors\\n    (Min-K%, ConStat) on these 10-15 models individually to show per-model degradation.\\nTwo-level validation: - Group level: DIF flags same items regardless of cohort composition (a/b/c) → robust - Model level: traditional detectors degrade on (c) individuals → fragile This design works even with only 10-15 reasoning-RL models because DIF doesn't need N≥80 RL models — it needs N≥80 in the cohort total, and RL models are mixed in.       "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"experiments\": [{\"id\": \"plan_001\", \"name\": \"real_data_dif_mmlu\", \"claim\": 1, \"description\": \"Apply DIF analysis to METABENCH MMLU response matrix (5,219 models × 12,508 items). Design: (a) Primary cohort: temporal split (2023 vs 2024-H1, both N≥80 ✓) (b) Alternative cohort: accuracy-quintile split (ability-based grouping, uses all models) (c) Item filtering: run with and without negative-discrimination items (sensitivity analysis) Run ETS C DIF detection on all items under both cohort definitions. Proxy validation against Li et al. (EMNLP 2024) web-overlap contamination labels (9,229 items, 73.8% coverage). IMPORTANT conceptual distinction: Li et al. labels are model-agnostic (item exists on web), while DIF detects cohort-differential contamination (item behaves differently across temporal cohorts). 1	# Paper Framing — irt_equated_bench_regen
```
```text
2	
3	## Target Venue
4	- Primary: EMNLP 2026 (via ARR; long paper 8-page limit + unlimited refs)
5	- Page limit: 8 pages main text (当前 10 页，需压缩 2 页)
6	
7	## Paper Type
8	Empirical analysis / psychometric characterization. Discovery paper, not methods paper. "DIF reveals benchmark structural problems," not "DIF detects contamination."
9	
10	## Core Claims (4 条, 可证伪)
11	
12	**Claim 1 (Temporal Comparability Concern)**: ~31% of MMLU items are functionally unstable across model generations — they exhibit large DIF (ETS C, |Δ_MH|≥1.5) between 2023 and 2024-H1 model cohorts. Primary estimate: **31.3%** (temporal cohort, plan_001). All 57 subjects show instability (C% range 20.7–48.4%, unweighted mean ≈30%, plan_001 domain_specificity.csv). Within-subject matching on 5 high-C% subjects yields higher rates (31-45%, avg 38.1%, plan_014) — report as robustness check, not primary. This instability is not a statistical artifact (random split sanity check: 0.18%), and correlates weakly with domain-specific accuracy improvement (ρ=0.307, p=0.02), but item-level decomposition shows domain improvement explains only 2.4pp (7.6%). Architecture heterogeneity is a non-issue: METABENCH MMLU is 99.98% decoder-only (5226/5227), excluding the 1 non-decoder model changes nothing (Jaccard=1.0). Threshold sensitivity robustness confirmed: C% ranges from 48.9% (|Δ_MH|≥1.0) to 18.3% (|Δ_MH|≥2.0) to 10.2% (|Δ_MH|≥2.5). BH FDR correction at q=0.05 has negligible impact (removes 3/3,918 items).
13	- Evidence: plan_001 (C%=31.3%), plan_014 (within-subject C%=38.1%), plan_010 (arch-stratified: 99.98% decoder-only, C% unchanged, Jaccard=1.0), plan_018 (threshold: 49/31/18/10% at 1.0/1.5/2.0/2.5; BH negligible), plan_019 (decomposition: ≤2.4pp/7.6% explained by domain improvement), plan_022 (model independence: graduated de-duplication to 1 model per family → C% ≤ random subsample control at every cap K; K=20: 29.1% vs random 30.9%, K=5: 11.7% vs random 16.9%; family structure does NOT inflate C%), plan_027 (temporal ability overlap: d=-0.17, OVL=76.4%; 1:1 NN matching C%=28.9%±0.1% vs full 31.3% — ability confound explains only 2.4pp; contrast with Llama-2v3 d=-0.99→matched C%=28.3%, where ability confound explains 33pp)
14	- Honesty requirement: ρ=0.307 (p=0.02) at subject level reflects a modest ecological correlation. Item-level decomposition (plan_019) shows domain improvement accounts for at most 2.4pp of 31.3% C% (7.6%; pseudo R²=0.0007). The instability is predominantly item-specific. Caveat: item difficulty may be a mediator rather than confounder — use Model 1 as primary; Model 2 (Simpson's paradox) as supplementary.
15	- IRT fit: 98% of models show |Lz|>2, reflecting MMLU's inherent multidimensionality (57 subjects), not architecture heterogeneity. 1D IRT is fundamentally misspecified for MMLU — discuss in limitations as motivation for future multidimensional IRT work.
16	
17	**Claim 2 (Ground Truth Meta-critique)**: Existing contamination detection literature validates against web-overlap/n-gram labels that measure global exposure (whether an item is publicly accessible), not differential contamination (whether an item functions differently across model cohorts). DIF enrichment≈1.0 against Li et al. labels is expected, not failure — these labels cannot evaluate item-level differential detectors. This is a fundamental validation methodology gap: all work using web-overlap labels as ground truth for item-level detectors (including Min-K%, n-gram methods) has the same blind spot. **Empirically validated** (plan_026): direct comparison of DIF-C flags vs Li et al. web-overlap labels on 9,229 MMLU items shows enrichment=0.964, Jaccard=0.206, Phi=0.012, χ²=1.28 (p=0.258), OR=1.05 [0.96, 1.15]. Consistent across subtypes (input_only: 0.986, input_and_label: 0.960). Semi-synthetic injection confirms DIF can detect differential contamination (ΔTPR=+0.508 at p=0.5, dose-response from p=0.3 to p=1.0).
18	- Evidence: plan_001 (enrichment=0.96), plan_026 (direct comparison: enrichment=0.964, Jaccard=0.206, p=0.258), plan_011 (ΔTPR=+0.508, dose-response)
19	- Framing: aggressive — "the field's standard validation approach is fundamentally mismatched for item-level detection" — now with direct empirical evidence, not just conceptual argument
20	
21	**Claim 3 (Semi-synthetic Validation Framework)**: The ΔTPR methodology — injecting known differential contamination into real response matrices and measuring detection power increase — provides a controlled validation infrastructure for any item-level contamination detector. Cross-subject matching (using total score on OTHER subjects as MH matching variable) eliminates FPR inflation (ΔFPR=0.000 across all 5 tested subjects), while within-subject matching suffers from matching variable contamination (FPR inflation +0.186).
22	- Evidence: plan_011, plan_016 (ΔFPR=0.000 cross-subject, 4/5 subjects ΔTPR>0.3), plan_004 Step 2 (GSM8K ΔTPR=+0.818 standard, +0.517 external)
23	- Cross-benchmark: GSM8K replication confirms framework portability to single-domain math benchmarks
24	- Positioning: cross-subject matching is a practical methodological recommendation; cross-benchmark replication strengthens generalizability
25	
26	**Claim 4 (Downstream Impact — Four-Layer Finding)**: DIF as diagnostic lens, not quality filter. Four sub-findings:
27	1. **Benchmark redundancy**: ρ≈0.997 between full and stable-only rankings — MMLU rankings are extremely robust to 31% item removal, revealing massive measurement redundancy in the benchmark.
28	2. **DIF items carry cross-cohort signal but are NOT the most ranking-defining items**: DIF removal causes MORE disruption than random removal (z=-8 to -49σ, 21/21 conditions), confirming DIF items are non-trivial. But circularity ablation (plan_020) shows DIF is the LEAST disruptive non-random strategy: accuracy-gap removal disrupts 10-16× more (z=-157 vs -9.5), high-variance 11× more (z=-108), high-discrimination 1.7× more (z=-16). MH's ability conditioning filters out raw accuracy differences, isolating genuine differential functioning rather than generally informative items. DIF-discrimination overlap is only 22.1% (Jaccard=0.12) — DIF identifies "items that changed across generations," not "good items."
29	3. **DIF as diagnostic lens**: DIF identifies which items' functioning changes most across model generations → reveals which capability dimensions drive ranking shifts. Not "find bad items and remove" but "understand what changed and why." Model-specific patterns: phi-3/llama-3 gain +700-850 ranks from DIF items, mistral merges lose -400 to -587. **Item characterization** (plan_023): DIF-C items are moderately harder (Cohen's d=-0.247) and higher-discrimination (d=0.230) than non-DIF items, but uniformly distributed across MMLU domains (χ² p=0.32). Key finding: difficulty × DIF direction interaction (ρ=-0.597) — hardest-decile DIF-C items are 88.7% focal-favoring (2024 models do better), easiest-decile are 96.2% reference-favoring (2023 models do better). This reveals a systematic pattern: capability improvement concentrates on hard items, while easy items' difficulty structure erodes across generations.
30	4. **Cross-benchmark validation (exploratory)**: On GSM8K (1,319 items × 6,702 models), DIF flags anti-correlate with GSM1K contamination gap (r=−0.478, p=0.005, N=33). **DEMOTED to exploratory** per Dream #6 three-tier consensus: N=33, CI wide [−0.71, −0.16], not strong enough for numbered contribution. This evidence is suggestive but should NOT appear in abstract/conclusion as core support.
31	- Evidence: plan_017 (z=-8 to -49σ, ρ=0.996-0.999), plan_020 (circularity ablation: DIF z=-9.5 vs acc-gap z=-157, disc z=-16, var z=-108), plan_023 (item characterization: difficulty ρ=-0.597, domain uniformity χ² p=0.32)
32	- Architecture: plan_017 is load-bearing (strong, full N=5,227). plan_020 circularity ablation is the second pillar. GSM1K correlation (plan_004 Step 3) is supplementary/exploratory only.
33	- Growing Pains互补: "Growing Pains uses anchor items for efficient evaluation; our DIF diagnostic tells them which anchor items are degrading"
34	- Caveats (→ limitations): N=33, Pearson CI upper bound |r|=0.155 (small effect possible), Spearman CI upper bound |ρ|=0.031 (near zero possible); item difficulty confound not fully excluded (DIF-C items harder for all models); contamination gap is proxy, not ground truth; replication with larger matched sample needed
35	
36	## Story Spine (v2 — no-pivot narrative)
37	
38	**Opening frame**: LLM benchmarks implicitly assume temporal score comparability — when we say "Model B outperforms Model A on MMLU," we presume both scores reflect the same latent construct. But do they? Psychometrics has a mature tool for testing exactly this: Differential Item Functioning (DIF) analysis detects items whose difficulty changes across examinee groups after controlling for overall ability. We apply MH-DIF to the METABENCH response matrix (5,227 models × 12,508 MMLU items) — the largest-scale DIF analysis on LLM benchmarks to date.
39	
40	**Key narrative principle**: "Instability ≠ invalidity." DIF flags items whose functioning changed across generations — this is a diagnostic signal, not a quality verdict. MMLU rankings are massively redundant (ρ≈0.997 after removing 31% of items), so instability doesn't threaten validity. But it reveals WHICH capability dimensions are shifting. **This framing must appear in the Introduction, not be deferred to Discussion.**
41	
42	**What we find** (4 contributions, focused):
43	
44	1. **31% of MMLU items are functionally unstable** across model generations (ETS C, |Δ_MH|≥1.5). Robust to threshold choice (49%/31%/18%/10% at 1.0/1.5/2.0/2.5), multiple testing (BH removes 3/3918 items), and architecture composition (99.98% decoder-only). Domain accuracy improvement explains ≤2.4pp of 31.3% C% (7.6%; pseudo R²=0.0007). The instability is predominantly item-specific, not a proxy for "models got better."
45	
46	2. **Ground truth meta-critique**: Web-overlap contamination labels measure global exposure, not differential contamination. Enrichment≈1.0 is expected. Semi-synthetic injection confirms DIF detects differential contamination (ΔTPR=+0.508). Any item-level detector validated against web-overlap labels has this blind spot.
47	
48	3. **Semi-synthetic validation framework**: ΔTPR methodology with cross-subject matching (ΔFPR=0.000). Portable across benchmarks (GSM8K ΔTPR=+0.818). Reusable for validating any item-level contamination detector.
49	
50	4. **DIF as diagnostic lens + actionable protocol**: DIF items carry cross-cohort signal (z=-8 to -49σ vs random) but are the LEAST disruptive non-random strategy (z=-9.5 vs accuracy-gap z=-157). DIF isolates genuine differential functioning, not just informative items. **Actionable contribution**: DIF audit protocol for benchmark maintainers (monitoring frequency, decision rules, response options).
51	
52	**Headline result**: "Psychometric DIF analysis of 5,227 models × 12,508 MMLU items reveals 31% temporal instability — robust across thresholds, not driven by capability improvement (≤7.6%), and not a validity threat (ρ≈0.997 ranking stability). The field's web-overlap ground truth cannot capture this differential signal; we provide a semi-synthetic validation framework with cross-subject matching (ΔFPR=0.000). DIF serves as a diagnostic lens for understanding what changed across model generations, with a concrete audit protocol for benchmark maintenance."
53	
54	## Scope Control (main body vs appendix)
55	
56	**MAIN BODY (4 claims + robustness)**:
57	- §1 Introduction (problem + "instability ≠ invalidity" framing upfront)
58	- §2 Related Work (45 papers, 5 clusters)
59	- §3 Method (MH-DIF primer, semi-synthetic framework, cross-subject matching)
60	- §4 Experiments: Claim 1 (temporal DIF + threshold + BH + decomposition + architecture), Claim 2 (enrichment + meta-critique), Claim 3 (semi-synthetic + cross-benchmark), Claim 4 (redundancy + circularity ablation + diagnostic lens + GSM1K validation)
61	- §5 Discussion + Limitations
62	- §6 Conclusion
63	
64	**APPENDIX (pruned from main body)**:
65	- Bayesian cumulative DIF (plan_007) — simulation-only, underdeveloped
66	- RL robustness (plan_002) — N=13, underpowered
67	- Ensemble fusion (plan_003a/b/c) — negative, not executed
68	- Bifactor equating pilot — below target fit
69	- Item generation pilot — below benchmarks
70	- Graceful degradation — clean but supplementary
71	- Item-Focused Trees — robustness check, supplementary
72	- ConStat/DVD comparisons — complementary baselines
73	
74	## Empirical Observation: Structural Constraints on DIF in LLM Benchmarks
75	
76	Systematic experimentation reveals that three structural improvements — matched-ability cohort design (plan_001b), IRT θ-conditioning (plan_012, r=0.9986), and bifactor pre-check (plan_014, within-subject C%=38.1%) — all fail to reduce temporal DIF baseline FPR below 31% while maintaining detection power. This is an empirical observation, not a formal impossibility result. The constraints arise from: (a) unidimensional IRT limitations in modeling 57-subject multidimensional ability, (b) statistical power requirements (N≥80, δ≥1.4), and (c) inability to separate ability confounding from genuine item-level changes. Higher-level methods (MIMIC, propensity-score matching, multidimensional IRT) may address individual constraints — future work.
77	
78	## Venue Fit
79	
80	- **EMNLP 2026**: Strong fit — NLP evaluation methodology is a dedicated area, reviewers expect empirical studies of benchmarks. "Empirical, not algorithmic" contribution type well-received. Risk: 8-page limit requires significant compression (current 10 pages). Mitigation: move structural analysis details to appendix.
81	
82	## Novelty Articulation (三层，显式化)
83	
84	MH-DIF (1959/1988) is applied without modification. **Explicitly acknowledge this.** Novelty is NOT algorithmic. Three layers of genuine contribution:
85	
86	1. **Empirical discovery at unprecedented scale**: First DIF analysis on 5,227×12,508 LLM response matrix. Finding: 31% temporal instability is robust across thresholds (10-49%), not explained by capability improvement (≤7.6%), and architecturally homogeneous (99.98% decoder-only). No prior work has characterized item-level stability of LLM benchmarks at this scale. The instability finding itself is new data about MMLU's measurement properties.
87	
88	2. **Ground truth meta-critique with field-wide implications**: The observation that web-overlap labels cannot validate item-level differential detectors is NOT obvious — the field routinely uses them. This affects the validity claims of Min-K%, ConStat, n-gram methods, and all papers using web-overlap as ground truth. First empirical demonstration (enrichment≈1.0 + semi-synthetic proof).
89	
90	3. **Semi-synthetic validation framework + cross-subject matching**: The ΔTPR methodology is a reusable infrastructure contribution. Cross-subject matching (ΔFPR=0.000) is a novel practical innovation — the insight that within-subject matching contaminates the conditioning variable is subtle and general. Portable to any multi-domain benchmark (demonstrated on GSM8K).
91	
92	**Framing**: "We bring mature psychometric tools to characterize an urgent ML evaluation problem. The contribution is the empirical findings, the validation methodology critique, and the reusable framework — not a new algorithm."
93	
94	## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns (updated for pivot)
95	
96	1. **"31% could just mean models got better / ability confound inflates C%"** — ✅ RESOLVED (plan_019 + plan_027). Item-level decomposition shows domain improvement explains ≤2.4pp of 31.3% C% (7.6%; pseudo R²=0.0007). Temporal cohort ability gap is negligible (d=-0.17, OVL=76.4%); 1:1 NN matching (d≈-0.01) yields C%=28.9%±0.1% vs full 31.3% — ability confound explains only 2.4pp. Contrast: Llama-2v3 (d=-0.99) drops from 61.3% to 28.3% under matching. Both converge to ~28-29% genuine item-level DIF.
97	
98	2. **"Ground truth critique is obvious — of course web labels don't capture differential effects"** — Not obvious: the field routinely uses these labels for item-level validation. We provide the first empirical demonstration with both the enrichment≈1.0 result and the semi-synthetic proof of concept.
99	
100	3. **"Semi-synthetic injection is artificial"** — We inject into real response matrices (not simulated), preserving real ability distributions and item characteristics. The mechanism (incorrect→correct flipping) is a simplification acknowledged in limitations.
101	
102	4. **"DIF power is only sufficient for large effects"** — Large effects are precisely what threaten benchmark validity most. Operational region: N≥80, δ≥1.4, TPR=0.68-0.80.
103	
104	5. **"Only validated on MMLU"** — Partially addressed: GSM8K replication (METABENCH 1,319 items × 6,702 models) confirms semi-synthetic framework portability (ΔTPR=+0.818) and temporal DIF pattern (C%=18.9%). **plan_028 in progress**: checking METABENCH for ARC/HellaSwag/WinoGrande/TruthfulQA response matrices to run multi-benchmark temporal DIF. Full generalization to diverse benchmarks remains future work if METABENCH lacks other benchmarks.
105	
106	6. **"Higher methods (MIMIC, propensity-score) could solve the confounding"** — Acknowledged as future work. We frame our three-attempt negative results as "empirical observation" that motivates these directions, not as impossibility.
107	
108	7. **"ETS threshold is uncalibrated for LLM populations"** — ✅ RESOLVED (plan_018). C% at {1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 2.5} = {48.9%, 31.3%, 18.3%, 10.2%}. Finding >15% holds at standard thresholds [1.0, 2.0]. At conservative 2.5, still 10.2% (1,278 items). Present as: "finding is robust across standard thresholds; even at the most conservative cutoff, >10% of items show large DIF." Do NOT claim ">15% at all thresholds" — be honest that 2.5 drops below.
109	
110	8. **"Multiple testing: 12,508 simultaneous tests without correction"** — ✅ RESOLVED (plan_018). BH FDR correction at q=0.05 removes only 3/3,918 ETS C items (31.32%→31.30%). At higher thresholds, BH impact is zero. ETS dual requirement (effect size + significance) provides near-complete implicit protection. Random-split sanity check (C%=0.18%) confirms empirically.
111	
112	9. **"Only uniform DIF — non-uniform DIF plausible for LLMs"** — ✅ RESOLVED (plan_021). LR-DIF on 9 subjects (1787 items): MH C-class 92.1% concordance with LR uniform DIF → MH detection robust. Non-uniform DIF: 38.7% at significance threshold, **24.8% at |β_interaction|≥0.5** (conservative, effect-size controlled). MH structurally underestimates instability. Caveat: LR used within-subject matching (vs MH's cross-benchmark), so 38.7% may be inflated; use 24.8% in paper. Framing: "our 31% uniform-only estimate is conservative."
113	
114	10. **"5,227 models are not independent — fine-tunes/merges inflate N and DIF significance"** — ✅ RESOLVED (plan_022). Graduated de-duplication (capping models per family at K=1,2,5,10,20) shows C% ≤ random subsample control at every cap: K=20 (447 models) C%=29.1% vs random 30.9%; K=5 (146 models) C%=11.7% vs random 16.9%; K=1 (33 families) C%=0.17% (underpowered, N<80). Family structure does NOT inflate C% — if anything, de-duplication reduces it slightly less than random subsampling. The 31.3% figure is robust to model non-independence.
115	
116	11. **"Ranking disruption result is tautological"** — ✅ RESOLVED (plan_020). Circularity ablation shows DIF removal is the LEAST disruptive non-random strategy: z=-9.5 vs accuracy-gap z=-157, discrimination z=-16, variance z=-108. Despite 62.6% item overlap with accuracy-gap selection, MH ability conditioning makes DIF 10-16× less disruptive. DIF isolates genuine differential functioning, not just items with large raw group differences. Tautology refuted.
117	
118	## Dropped from Original Framing
119	
120	- **Ensemble (Claim 2 old)**: plan_003a/b/c never executed. Future work.
121	- **Regeneration (Claim 3 old)**: Bifactor killed by plan_014 pre-check. Dropped entirely.
122	- **RL robustness**: Theoretically motivated but N=13 insufficient. Limitations disclosure, not claim.
123	- **Detection as primary contribution**: Pivoted from "we detect contamination" to "we characterize benchmark properties."
124	
```

Finding: 31% temporal instability is robust across thresholds (10-49%), not explained by capability improvement (≤7.6%), and architecturally homogeneous (99.98% decoder-only). No prior work has characterized item-level stability of LLM benchmarks at this scale. The instability finding itself is new data about MMLU's measurement properties.\n\n2. **Ground truth meta-critique with field-wide implications**: The observation that web-overlap labels cannot validate item-level differential detectors is NOT obvious — the field routinely uses them. This affects the validity claims of Min-K%, ConStat, n-gram methods, and all papers using web-overlap as ground truth. First empirical demonstration (enrichment≈1.0 + semi-synthetic proof).\n\n3. **Semi-synthetic validation framework + cross-subject matching**: The ΔTPR methodology is a reusable infrastructure contribution. Cross-subject matching (ΔFPR=0.000) is a novel practical innovation — the insight that within-subject matching contaminates the conditioning variable is subtle and general. Portable to any multi-domain benchmark (demonstrated on GSM8K).\n\n**Framing**: \"We bring mature psychometric tools to characterize an urgent ML evaluation problem. The contribution is the empirical findings, the validation methodology critique, and the reusable framework — not a new algorithm.\"\n\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns (updated for pivot)\n\n1. **\"31% could just mean models got better / ability confound inflates C%\"** — ✅ RESOLVED (plan_019 + plan_027). Item-level decomposition shows domain improvement explains ≤2.4pp of 31.3% C% (7.6%; pseudo R²=0.0007). Temporal cohort ability gap is negligible (d=-0.17, OVL=76.4%); 1:1 NN matching (d≈-0.01) yields C%=28.9%±0.1% vs full 31.3% — ability confound explains only 2.4pp. Contrast: Llama-2v3 (d=-0.99) drops from 61.3% to 28.3% under matching. Both converge to ~28-29% genuine item-level DIF.\n\n2. **\"Ground truth critique is obvious — of course web labels don't capture differential effects\"** — Not obvious: the field routinely uses these labels for item-level validation. We provide the first empirical demonstration with both the enrichment≈1.0 result and the semi-synthetic proof of concept.\n\n3. **\"Semi-synthetic injection is artificial\"** — We inject into real response matrices (not simulated), preserving real ability distributions and item characteristics. The mechanism (incorrect→correct flipping) is a simplification acknowledged in limitations.\n\n4. **\"DIF power is only sufficient for large effects\"** — Large effects are precisely what threaten benchmark validity most. Operational region: N≥80, δ≥1.4, TPR=0.68-0.80.\n\n5. **\"Only validated on MMLU\"** — Partially addressed: GSM8K replication (METABENCH 1,319 items × 6,702 models) confirms semi-synthetic framework portability (ΔTPR=+0.818) and temporal DIF pattern (C%=18.9%). **plan_028 in progress**: checking METABENCH for ARC/HellaSwag/WinoGrande/TruthfulQA response matrices to run multi-benchmark temporal DIF. Full generalization to diverse benchmarks remains future work if METABENCH lacks other benchmarks.\n\n6. **\"Higher methods (MIMIC, propensity-score) could solve the confounding\"** — Acknowledged as future work. We frame our three-attempt negative results as \"empirical observation\" that motivates these directions, not as impossibility.\n\n7. **\"ETS threshold is uncalibrated for LLM populations\"** — ✅ RESOLVED (plan_018). C% at {1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 2.5} = {48.9%, 31.3%, 18.3%, 10.2%}. Finding >15% holds at standard thresholds [1.0, 2.0]. At conservative 2.5, still 10.2% (1,278 items). Present as: \"finding is robust across standard thresholds; even at the most conservative cutoff, >10% of items show large DIF.\" Do NOT claim \">15% at all thresholds\" — be honest that 2.5 drops below.\n\n8. **\"Multiple testing: 12,508 simultaneous tests without correction\"** — ✅ RESOLVED (plan_018). BH FDR correction at q=0.05 removes only 3/3,918 ETS C items (31.32%→31.30%). At higher thresholds, BH impact is zero. ETS dual requirement (effect size + significance) provides near-complete implicit protection. Random-split sanity check (C%=0.18%) confirms empirically.\n\n9. **\"Only uniform DIF — non-uniform DIF plausible for LLMs\"** — ✅ RESOLVED (plan_021). LR-DIF on 9 subjects (1787 items): MH C-class 92.1% concordance with LR uniform DIF → MH detection robust. Non-uniform DIF: 38.7% at significance threshold, **24.8% at |β_interaction|≥0.5** (conservative, effect-size controlled). MH structurally underestimates instability. Caveat: LR used within-subject matching (vs MH's cross-benchmark), so 38.7% may be inflated; use 24.8% in paper. Framing: \"our 31% uniform-only estimate is conservative.\"\n\n10. **\"5,227 models are not independent — fine-tunes/merges inflate N and DIF significance\"** — ✅ RESOLVED (plan_022). Graduated de-duplication (capping models per family at K=1,2,5,10,20) shows C% ≤ random subsample control at every cap: K=20 (447 models) C%=29.1% vs random 30.9%; K=5 (146 models) C%=11.7% vs random 16.9%; K=1 (33 families) C%=0.17% (underpowered, N<80). Family structure does NOT inflate C% — if anything, de-duplication reduces it slightly less than random subsampling. The 31.3% figure is robust to model non-independence.\n\n11. **\"Ranking disruption result is tautological\"** — ✅ RESOLVED (plan_020). Circularity ablation shows DIF removal is the LEAST disruptive non-random strategy: z=-9.5 vs accuracy-gap z=-157, discrimination z=-16, variance z=-108. Despite 62.6% item overlap with accuracy-gap selection, MH ability conditioning makes DIF 10-16× less disruptive. DIF isolates genuine differential functioning, not just items with large raw group differences. Tautology refuted.\n\n## Dropped from Original Framing\n\n- **Ensemble (Claim 2 old)**: plan_003a/b/c never executed. Future work.\n- **Regeneration (Claim 3 old)**: Bifactor killed by plan_014 pre-check. Dropped entirely.\n- **RL robustness**: Theoretically motivated but N=13 insufficient. Limitations disclosure, not claim.\n- **Detection as primary contribution**: Pivoted from \"we detect contamination\" to \"we characterize benchmark properties.\"\n",

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I have a comprehensive picture. Let me analyze the current experimental metrics and provide an honest assessment. Let me also read the dream reports again (they're in the system reminders).

Let me compile the analysis:

## Current Experimental Metrics

### Core Results (Completed):
1. **plan_001**: MMLU C%=31.3% (5,227 models × 12,508 items)
2. **plan_028**: 6-benchmark cross-validation: MMLU 31.3%, WinoGrande 30.3%, TruthfulQA 29.7%, ARC-Challenge 26.9%, GSM8K 18.9%, HellaSwag 17.9% — all ≥45× above random controls


3. **plan_030**: Simpson's paradox showing OR reversal from 1.55 to 0.73
4. **plan_011**: Semi-synthetic validation with no false positive rate change but true positive rate improvement of 50.8%
5. **plan_029**: Permutation null test yielding z=388.5
6. **plan_031**: 6-month window analysis showing C%=34.35% with 181× enrichment
7. **plan_032**: DIF-cleaned MMLU revealing 89.5% of models shift by at least 5 ranks, 2024 rank change of +73.6, and random baseline z=8.5
8. **plan_033**: Subject-specific DIF patterns where STEM favors 2024 models at 55.4% while Humanities favors 2023 at 64.4%, plus bootstrap confidence intervals for MMLU C% at 31.3% [29.5%, 33.1%] across 1000 iterations. I'm also examining architectural composition (nearly all decoder-only), threshold sensitivity across different cutoffs, model independence through graduated deduplication, ability overlap metrics, within-family comparisons, non-uniform DIF patterns with 92.1% concordance, and circularity checks showing DIF has minimal disruptive effect compared to accuracy gaps.

Now I'm looking at Dream #7 feedback scores ranging from 2.5 to 3.5, with reviewers identifying structural weaknesses around temporal resolution, causal ambiguity, and synthetic data limitations—all of which I've addressed through revised language, expanded methodology sections, and additional citations. The best-tier reviewers want detector comparisons and code release, which I've partially handled by including ConStat metrics in the main text.

The real strength here is the scale and rigor: over 5,000 models tested across 27,000 items with extensive controls for confounds, plus the Simpson's paradox finding and algebraic guarantees on cross-subject matching. But the novelty ceiling is the main barrier to a 4.0—the core contribution relies on applying existing DIF methodology rather than introducing fundamentally new techniques.

Beyond that, there are some practical gaps: the detector comparison lacks a direct head-to-head evaluation of competing methods on identical items, and while the paradox resolution helps, the 89.5% shift in rankings is meaningful but not transformative. The causal ambiguity is structural—MH-DIF can't inherently distinguish whether observed differences stem from contamination or genuine capability shifts, which is a limitation that careful framing can acknowledge but not overcome.

For a 3.5 rating, I'm quite confident given the 28 experiments, six benchmarks, rigorous controls, and honest framing. The real question is whether we can push toward 4.0, which faces a novelty ceiling: the core method dates to 1959, and applying it to a new domain, while valuable, doesn't fully escape that constraint. The tier-one feedback was explicit—absence of true detector comparison and primarily empirical application are the limiting factors. We've partially addressed the first but haven't run a genuine Min-K%/DVD comparison, and while Proposition 1 and the subject direction asymmetry add some novelty, the work remains fundamentally empirical in nature.

The ceiling depends heavily on the reviewer's priorities: if they weight novelty heavily, we're looking at 3.5; if they value empirical rigor and insight, 4.0 is possible. The paper's quality in writing, controls, and robustness is already near its limit. I should wait for the Dream #8 results—that's the real deciding factor.
```

```json
  "content": "[审稿报告]\nOverall: 3.5\n\n# 审稿报告 — Round 5 (Fresh Review)\n\n**论文**: Psychometric Instability of LLM Benchmarks: Differential Item Functioning Reveals 31% of MMLU Items Behave Differently Across Model Generations\n**目标 Venue**: EMNLP 2026 (via ACL Rolling Review)\n**截止日期**: 2026-05-25 AoE\n**审稿模式**: Fresh (重新审稿，2 名 Reviewer，1 轮 + 交叉回应)\n**日期**: 2026-05-21\n\n---\n\n## Summary\n\n本文将教育测量中的 Mantel-Haenszel Differential Item Functioning (MH-DIF) 分析大规模应用于 LLM benchmark 评估，基于 METABENCH 数据集（5,227 models × 12,508 MMLU items）发现 31.3% 的 MMLU items 在 2023 vs 2024 模型代际间表现出 ETS Category C 级别的 DIF。跨 6 个基准复现（C% = 17.9–31.3%，45–174× 高于随机基线）。提出 cross-subject matching 的半合成验证框架（ΔFPR = 0.000），并发现 DIF flags 与 web-overlap contamination labels 近乎独立（enrichment ≈ 0.964）。论文声称 4 个贡献：temporal comparability（Claim 1）、ground truth meta-critique（Claim 2）、semi-synthetic validation framework（Claim 3）、DIF as diagnostic lens（Claim 4）。\n\n---\n\n## Strengths\n\n1. **大规模实证贡献**：5,227 models × 27,125 items 的 DIF 分析规模在该领域最大之一；6 个 benchmark 交叉验证提供了强力 replication。31% item instability 对 benchmark 使用者是有直接意义的发现。\n\n2. **系统性 confound 控制**：5 种 cohort design（temporal, ability-quintile, within-family, within-subject, random-split）系统排除替代解释。Matched-ability 分析令人信服地将 ability confounding 限定在 ~2.4pp（Cohen's d = -0.17, matched C% = 28.9% ± 0.1%）。\n\n3. **Ground truth meta-critique 有实际价值**：Claim 2 发现 DIF flags 与 web-overlap labels 几乎独立（enrichment = 0.964, Jaccard = 0.206, φ = 0.012），对 contamination detection 文献中普遍使用 web-overlap 作为 ground truth 的做法提出了合理质疑。\n\n4. **跨学科 transplant 质量高**：将 60+ 年历史的 psychometric DIF 方法引入 NLP evaluation 是有价值的 cross-pollination。执行严谨（threshold sensitivity, multiple testing, architecture homogeneity check）。Figure 1 直观展示 global vs. differential contamination 的区别。\n\n5. **写作整体清晰**：结构合理，方法描述详尽，论文在 8 页内组织了大量实证内容。Limitations 涵盖全面。\n\n---\n\n## Weaknesses（按严重程度排序）\n\n### Must-Fix（影响接收决定）\n\n**MF-1** [critical, consensus] **Semi-synthetic framework 仅验证 single-subject injection，生态效度不足**\n- Proposition 1（ΔFPR = 0）是 by construction 的平凡结果：注入 subject X → matching variable 排除 X → 信号数学上无法泄漏。在现实场景中，contamination 同时跨越多个 subjects，此时 cross-subject matching variable 本身被污染，conditional independence 假设不再成立。论文未测试 multi-subject simultaneous injection（k ∈ {2,3,5,10}），因此无法评估框架在现实条件下的有效性。这直接影响 Claim 3 能否作为独立贡献成立。\n- **R1 提出，R2 AGREE**。R2 补充：cross-benchmark validation（Section 4.7）隐含了多基准同时 DIF 的场景，但不等同于控制实验。\n- 建议：补充 multi-subject simultaneous injection 实验，或将 Claim 3 显式限定为 single-subject contamination detection。\n\n**MF-2** [critical, consensus] **核心 framing 的因果模糊性：\"Psychometric Instability\" vs. \"Expected Capability Evolution\"**\n- MH-DIF 标记 **任何** 跨组 conditional item functioning 差异，包括 genuine capability change。ρ = -0.597 的 difficulty-direction interaction（2024 模型难题 gain、简单题 erode）同样（甚至更自然地）可解释为正常的 capability profile shift。标题 \"Psychometric Instability\" 暗示 benchmark 测量问题，但如果 DIF 主要反映模型能力合理演变，则不是 \"instability\" 而是 \"expected differential performance\"。Calendar year cohort 同时混淆了 temporal drift、architecture shift、training data change，论文无法将 DIF signal 归因到具体原因。\n- **R1 提出 (N2)，R2 AGREE with nuance (N3)**。R2 补充：MH 已控制 ability level，Simpson's paradox 和三层控制提供部分反驳（C% after controlling for ability 仍为 31.3%），但 within-stratum profile shift 仍可以是 legitimate。\n- 建议：(1) 软化标题——改为 \"Differential Item Functioning\" 或 \"Measurement Drift\"；(2) 明确区分 item instability vs. differential performance；(3) 补充 within-year cohort（2024-H1 vs H2）的完整分析。\n\n**MF-3** [critical, consensus] **Claim 4 \"DIF as diagnostic lens\" 证据不足**\n- GSM1K correlation (r = -0.478, partial r = -0.385, N = 33, CI = [-0.71, -0.16]) 统计效力严重不足，单个异常模型即可改变结论。Claim 4 的另一支撑——difficulty-direction interaction（ρ = -0.597）——本身解读有歧义（见 MF-2）。论文在 Limitations 中承认 GSM1K 是 exploratory，但 Discussion Section 5.3 和 Introduction Claim 4 的措辞未匹配这一谨慎程度。4 个 contributions 中如果 1 个站不住，贡献度整体打折扣。\n- **R2 提出 (N1)，R1 AGREE (W4)**。\n- 建议：降级 Claim 4 为 exploratory observation，从 contribution list 中移除，收紧为 3 个 contributions。\n\n### Should-Fix（影响评分但不一票否决）\n\n**SF-1** [warning] **方法论新颖性论证不充分**\n- MH-DIF 是 1988 年方法，cross-subject matching 有先例（Nandakumar 1993, Donoghue 1993——论文自己承认）。4 个 claims 中 3 个本质是 descriptive findings。论文需更清晰论证 LLM-specific 的方法适配挑战（local dependence, model family structure, ability 分布非正态）。\n- R2 列为 critical，R1 REBUT → 降级为 Should-Fix（论文已 self-position 为 empirical contribution, Introduction L19）。R2 交叉回应后 Excitement 上调至 3.5/5。\n- 建议：Section 3 开头增加 1-2 段讨论 LLM 作为 examinees 的特殊性。\n\n**SF-2** [warning] **Bootstrap CI 报告不完整**\n- 95% CI = [29.4%, 38.1%]，宽 8.7pp，right-skewed。未报告 bootstrap resampling unit（model vs. item）。\n- R1 列为 critical (N3)，R2 REBUT → 降级为 Should-Fix（整个 CI 在 \"roughly one-third\" 范围，qualitative conclusion 不变）。\n- 建议：报告 resampling strategy；Abstract 加 \"approximately\" 或 CI。\n\n**SF-3** [warning] **DIF Audit Protocol (Section 5.4) 过于简略**\n- 仅 3 句话，对于声称 \"LLM benchmarks need psychometric monitoring\" 的论文，practical recommendations 深度不足。ρ ≈ 0.997 说明 aggregate ranking 不受影响，但 25% 模型 rank shift ≥ 100——何时需要 action vs. 只需 annotation 未讨论。\n- R2 提出 (W1)，R1 AGREE。\n\n**SF-4** [warning] **ΔFPR = 0.000 的 robustness check 不足**\n- 注入率固定 20%，仅 5 个 subjects 验证，未讨论 Type II error（cross-subject matching 是否过于保守降低 TPR）。\n- R2 提出 (W2)，R1 AGREE（与 MF-1 互补）。\n\n**SF-5** [warning] **K = 5 分层敏感性分析未在主文呈现**\n- Appendix K = 20 θ-conditioning FPR = 0.312 ≈ K = 5，但此关键信息埋在附录。应在主文对比 K = 5/10/20。\n- R1 提出 (W1)，R2 AGREE。\n\n**SF-6** [warning] **Yen's Q3 局部依赖对 C% 的 inflation 未量化**\n- 11.3% within-subject pairs 超 |Q3| > 0.20 是 non-trivial。应通过 simulation 估计此水平 LD 对 Type I error rate 的影响。\n- R1 提出 (W2)，R2 AGREE。\n\n**SF-7** [warning] **DIF 术语对 EMNLP 读者不够友好**\n- ETS Category A/B/C、α_MH、Δ_MH 对 NLP 社区陌生。缺少 running example。\n- R2 提出 (W3)，R1 AGREE。\n\n**SF-8** [warning] **与 Growing Pains 的区分缺乏实证验证**\n- 论文仅一句 \"our work is complementary\"，未验证 DIF-flagged items 与 FPC anchor items 的 overlap（Jaccard/enrichment）。\n- R2 提出 (W4)，R1 AGREE。\n\n**SF-9** [warning] **引用问题**\n- 8 个 orphan bib entries（chen2025irtnet, choi2026llmjudgeirt, li2025atlas, liang2023helm, mialon2023gaia, ott2022benchmark, wang2024mmlupro, zhang2024gsm1k）\n- holland1988mh year mismatch (bib=1988 vs possible 1986)\n- tao2026selfcritique, liu2026bhi 的 first author mismatch\n- R2 提出 (W5)，R1 AGREE。\n\n**SF-10** [warning] **Claim 2 (ground truth meta-critique) 逻辑可逆**\n- DIF ⊥ web-overlap 有两种等价解读：(A) web-overlap 是 wrong ground truth，或 (B) DIF 不检测 contamination。Semi-synthetic 部分化解了 B（ΔTPR > 0），但生态效度受限（见 MF-1）。\n- R1 提出 (W6)，R2 REBUT with nuance：semi-synthetic 证明 DIF 可以检测 contamination，是 interpretive gap 而非 methodological flaw。\n\n**SF-11** [warning] **Power analysis 假设不透明**\n- Section 4.1 声称 N ≥ 80 达到 TPR 0.68–0.80，但未报告 IRT 参数、effect size、simulation procedure。\n- R1 提出 (W5)，R2 AGREE。\n\n---\n\n## Reviewer Consensus\n\n以下问题两位 Reviewer 均认为需要改进（标注 [AGREE]）：\n\n1. **Claim 4 / GSM1K N = 33 不足以支撑独立 contribution** → 降级为 exploratory observation\n2. **Semi-synthetic framework 生态效度有限** → 需 multi-subject injection 或限定 scope\n3. **\"Psychometric Instability\" framing 过强** → 需软化 causal claims + 补充 within-year 分析\n4. **DIF Audit Protocol 过于简略** → 需扩展 actionable recommendations\n5. **K = 5 sensitivity 应在主文呈现**\n6. **Q3 局部依赖影响应量化**\n7. **DIF 术语需要 running example**\n8. **Growing Pains overlap 分析应补充**\n9. **引用需清理和修正**\n\n---\n\n## Reviewer Divergence\n\n以下问题存在分歧（已通过交叉回应解决或记录）：\n\n1. **Novelty 严重程度**：R2 列为 critical，R1 REBUT → 降级 Should-Fix。R1 论点：论文已 self-position 为 empirical contribution，EMNLP 接受此类工作，但需补充 LLM-specific challenges 讨论。R2 交叉回应后 Excitement 上调至 3.5/5，部分接受。\n\n2. **Bootstrap CI 严重程度**：R1 列为 critical，R2 REBUT → 降级 Should-Fix。R2 论点：CI 完全在 \"roughly one-third\" 范围内，qualitative conclusion 不变。\n\n3. **Within-family matched C% ≈ 28.3% 的解读**：R1 认为可能是 K = 5 下的 floor effect；R2 REBUT——K = 20 θ-conditioning 结果（FPR = 0.312）与 K = 5 无差别，反驳 floor effect 假说。分歧保留但非关键。\n\n4. **Claim 2 逻辑可逆性**：R1 认为是 Should-Fix；R2 认为 semi-synthetic 已部分化解，是 interpretive gap 而非 methodological flaw。R2 的 Soundness 4 → 3.5 的调整已考虑此因素。\n\n---\n\n## Questions（需要作者回应）\n\n1. Bootstrap resampling 的 unit 是什么（model-level vs. item-level）？不同策略下 CI 是否有实质变化？\n2. 如果同时对 k 个 subjects 注入 contamination，cross-subject matching 的 ΔFPR 如何随 k 变化？\n3. 论文如何回应 \"31% DIF 中有多少比例是 legitimate capability evolution 而非 measurement instability\" 的问题？是否有定量 decomposition 的可能性？\n4. Yen's Q3 的 11.3% within-subject violation rate 对 C% 的 expected inflation 量级是多少？\n5. DIF-flagged items 与 Growing Pains 的 FPC anchor items 的 overlap 是多少？\n\n---\n\n## Missing References\n\n两位 Reviewer 共同确认以下遗漏（应在 Related Work 中引用或讨论）：\n\n1. **\"Lifting the Benchmark Iceberg with Item-Response Theory\"** (2025, OpenReview) — IRT 揭示 benchmark 实现细节对排名的影响，与本文高度相关\n2. **\"Benchmark2: Systematic Evaluation of LLM Benchmarks\"** (2025, arXiv:2601.03986) — 系统评估 benchmark 质量的框架\n3. **ATLAS / CAT-based LLM evaluation** (Li et al. 2025, 已在 bib 但未引用) — Computerized Adaptive Testing 与 item-level diagnosis 相关\n4. **MedIRT / \"Measuring Competency, Not Performance\"** (2025, arXiv:2509.24186) — 将 IRT 应用于医学 benchmark\n\n此外，8 个 orphan bib entries 应清理。\n\n---\n\n## External Review (Stanford Agentic Reviewer)\n\n外部审稿：未配置。\n\n---\n\n## Visual Layout Check（非评分项）\n\n视觉审查已完成（2 warnings, 7 info）：\n\n**Warnings:**\n- **Pages 3–5**：连续 3 页无 figure/table（Figure 1 在 p2，下一个 Table 1 在 p6，间隔 4 页），p4-5 有 display equations 和 Proposition 1 缓解了单调感，但快速翻阅时视觉节奏偏平\n- **Pages 1–8**：主正文 8 页仅 4 个 display items（2 fig + 2 table），对实证论文偏低（典型 5–8 个）。建议将 Figure 4（downstream ranking impact）或 Table 3（structural controls）提升到主正文\n\n**Info:**\n- 首页无视觉锚点，Figure 1 在 p2 顶部，可接受\n- Figure 2 axis labels 字号偏小（约 60–65% 正文字号），建议 ≥ 70%\n- 末页 40% 空白可优化\n- Figure 4 散点图部分点重叠密集区辨识度降低\n- Tables 使用 booktabs，格式规范\n- 公式和 Proposition 排版正确\n- References 格式统一，无 [?] 未解析引用\n\n---\n\n## Overall Assessment\n\n**Overall Score: 3.5 / 5 — Borderline Conference**\n\n| 维度 | R1 | R2 | 综合 |\n|------|-----|-----|------|\n| Soundness | 3.5 | 3.5 | 3.5 |\n| Excitement | 3.5 | 3.5 | 3.5 |\n| Reproducibility | 4 | 4 | 4 |\n| Clarity | 4 | 4 | 4 |\n\n**置信度**: 4/5（两位 Reviewer 均仔细通读全文及附录，对 DIF 方法论和 LLM evaluation 文献熟悉）\n\n**判断依据**：\n- 这是一篇 well-executed 的大规模实证研究，核心发现（31% MMLU items 跨代不稳定）对 benchmark 使用者有直接价值\n- 6 个 benchmark 的交叉验证和 5 种 cohort design 的 confound 控制展示了扎实的方法论\n- 但存在 3 个 critical 问题：(1) semi-synthetic 框架生态效度不足（single-subject only），(2) 因果 framing 过强（instability vs. capability evolution 不可区分），(3) Claim 4 证据不足\n- 方法论新颖性有限——核心是 off-the-shelf psychometric method 的高质量应用，contributions 偏 descriptive\n- 如果 MF-1 至 MF-3 得到有效 address（特别是 MF-2 的 framing 软化和 MF-3 的 Claim 4 降级），论文有望上升到 4.0（Conference）水平\n- 当前定位：strong Findings / borderline Conference\n\n**EMNLP 2026 评分映射**：3.5 = Borderline Conference（可被主会议接收的边界线）\n\n**如果作者只能修复 3 个问题，优先级为**：\n1. 软化 causal framing + 标题（MF-2）— 成本低、影响大\n2. 补充 multi-subject injection Item Functioning\" 或 \"Measurement Drift\"\n- Abstract 数字过度密集（ρ≈0.997, z=-8σ to -49σ, ρ=-0.597 等），建议精简至 3-4 个核心数字\n- Abstract 中 \"31%\" 应加 \"approximately\" 或报告 CI\n\n### Section 1 (Introduction)\n- Claim 4 应降级或移除，收紧为 3 个 contributions\n- \"Our contributions are empirical and methodological, not algorithmic\" (L19) 的 self-positioning 是合适的，但后续 Method 部分未充分展开 LLM-specific challenges\n\n### Section 2 (Related Work)\n- DIF 方法论引用已较全面（Holland & Thayer 1988, Clauser 1998, Nandakumar 1993 等）\n- 与 Growing Pains 的区分过于简短——需补充 overlap 分析\n- 缺失 4 篇 2025-2026 重要相关工作（见 Missing References）\n\n### Section 3 (Method)\n- MH-DIF 描述准确但对 NLP 读者不够友好——需 running example\n- Proposition 1 表述应更谨慎——明确其仅适用于 single-subject injection\n- Cross-subject matching 的 prior art 定位（Nandakumar 1993, Donoghue 1993）已在文中，但应更明确是 [审稿报告]
```
Overall: 3.5

# 审稿报告 — Round 5 (Fresh Review)

**论文**: Psychometric Instability of LLM Benchmarks: Differential Item Functioning Reveals 31% of MMLU Items Behave Differently Across Model Generations
**目标 Venue**: EMNLP 2026 (via ACL Rolling Review)
**截止日期**: 2026-05-25 AoE
**审稿模式**: Fresh (重新审稿，2 名 Reviewer，1 轮 + 交叉回应)
**日期**: 2026-05-21

---

## Summary

本文将教育测量中的 Mantel-Haenszel Differential Item Functioning (MH-DIF) 分析大规模应用于 LLM benchmark 评估，基于 METABENCH 数据集（5,227 models × 12,508 MMLU items）发现 31.3% 的 MMLU items 在 2023 vs 2024 模型代际间表现出 ETS Category C 级别的 DIF。跨 6 个基准复现（C% = 17.9–31.3%，45–174× 高于随机基线）。提出 cross-subject matching 的半合成验证框架（ΔFPR = 0.000），并发现 DIF flags 与 web-overlap contamination labels 近乎独立（enrichment ≈ 0.964）。论文声称 4 个贡献：temporal comparability（Claim 1）、ground truth meta-critique（Claim 2）、semi-synthetic validation framework（Claim 3）、DIF as diagnostic lens（Claim 4）。

---

## Strengths

1. **大规模实证贡献**：5,227 models × 27,125 items 的 DIF 分析规模在该领域最大之一；6 个 benchmark 交叉验证提供了强力 replication。31% item instability 对 benchmark 使用者是有直接意义的发现。

2. **系统性 confound 控制**：5 种 cohort design（temporal, ability-quintile, within-family, within-subject, random-split）系统排除替代解释。Matched-ability 分析令人信服地将 ability confounding 限定在 ~2.4pp（Cohen's d = -0.17, matched C% = 28.9% ± 0.1%）。

3. **Ground truth meta-critique 有实际价值**：Claim 2 发现 DIF flags 与 web-overlap labels 几乎独立（enrichment = 0.964, Jaccard = 0.206, φ = 0.012），对 contamination detection 文献中普遍使用 web-overlap 作为 ground truth 的做法提出了合理质疑。

4. **跨学科 transplant 质量高**：将 60+ 年历史的 psychometric DIF 方法引入 NLP evaluation 是有价值的 cross-pollination。执行严谨（threshold sensitivity, multiple testing, architecture homogeneity check）。Figure 1 直观展示 global vs. differential contamination 的区别。

5. **写作整体清晰**：结构合理，方法描述详尽，论文在 8 页内组织了大量实证内容。Limitations 涵盖全面。

---

## Weaknesses（按严重程度排序）

### Must-Fix（影响接收决定）

**MF-1** [critical, consensus] **Semi-synthetic framework 仅验证 single-subject injection，生态效度不足**
- Proposition 1（ΔFPR = 0）是 by construction 的平凡结果：注入 subject X → matching variable 排除 X → 信号数学上无法泄漏。在现实场景中，contamination 同时跨越多个 subjects，此时 cross-subject matching variable 本身被污染，conditional independence 假设不再成立。论文未测试 multi-subject simultaneous injection（k ∈ {2,3,5,10}），因此无法评估框架在现实条件下的有效性。这直接影响 Claim 3 能否作为独立贡献成立。
- **R1 提出，R2 AGREE**。R2 补充：cross-benchmark validation（Section 4.7）隐含了多基准同时 DIF 的场景，但不等同于控制实验。
- 建议：补充 multi-subject simultaneous injection 实验，或将 Claim 3 显式限定为 single-subject contamination detection。

**MF-2** [critical, consensus] **核心 framing 的因果模糊性："Psychometric Instability" vs. "Expected Capability Evolution"**
- MH-DIF 标记 **任何** 跨组 conditional item functioning 差异，包括 genuine capability change。ρ = -0.597 的 difficulty-direction interaction（2024 模型难题 gain、简单题 erode）同样（甚至更自然地）可解释为正常的 capability profile shift。标题 "Psychometric Instability" 暗示 benchmark 测量问题，但如果 DIF 主要反映模型能力合理演变，则不是 "instability" 而是 "expected differential performance"。Calendar year cohort 同时混淆了 temporal drift、architecture shift、training data change，论文无法将 DIF signal 归因到具体原因。
- **R1 提出 (N2)，R2 AGREE with nuance (N3)**。R2 补充：MH 已控制 ability level，Simpson's paradox 和三层控制提供部分反驳（C% after controlling for ability 仍为 31.3%），但 within-stratum profile shift 仍可以是 legitimate。
- 建议：(1) 软化标题——改为 "Differential Item Functioning" 或 "Measurement Drift"；(2) 明确区分 item instability vs. differential performance；(3) 补充 within-year cohort（2024-H1 vs H2）的完整分析。

**MF-3** [critical, consensus] **Claim 4 "DIF as diagnostic lens" 证据不足**
- GSM1K correlation (r = -0.478, partial r = -0.385, N = 33, CI = [-0.71, -0.16]) 统计效力严重不足，单个异常模型即可改变结论。Claim 4 的另一支撑——difficulty-direction interaction（ρ = -0.597）——本身解读有歧义（见 MF-2）。论文在 Limitations 中承认 GSM1K 是 exploratory，但 Discussion Section 5.3 和 Introduction Claim 4 的措辞未匹配这一谨慎程度。4 个 contributions 中如果 1 个站不住，贡献度整体打折扣。
- **R2 提出 (N1)，R1 AGREE (W4)**。
- 建议：降级 Claim 4 为 exploratory observation，从 contribution list 中移除，收紧为 3 个 contributions。

### Should-Fix（影响评分但不一票否决）

**SF-1** [warning] **方法论新颖性论证不充分**
- MH-DIF 是 1988 年方法，cross-subject matching 有先例（Nandakumar 1993, Donoghue 1993——论文自己承认）。4 个 claims 中 3 个本质是 descriptive findings。论文需更清晰论证 LLM-specific 的方法适配挑战（local dependence, model family structure, ability 分布非正态）。
- R2 列为 critical，R1 REBUT → 降级为 Should-Fix（论文已 self-position 为 empirical contribution, Introduction L19）。R2 交叉回应后 Excitement 上调至 3.5/5。
- 建议：Section 3 开头增加 1-2 段讨论 LLM 作为 examinees 的特殊性。

**SF-2** [warning] **Bootstrap CI 报告不完整**
- 95% CI = [29.4%, 38.1%]，宽 8.7pp，right-skewed。未报告 bootstrap resampling unit（model vs. item）。
- R1 列为 critical (N3)，R2 REBUT → 降级为 Should-Fix（整个 CI 在 "roughly one-third" 范围，qualitative conclusion 不变）。
- 建议：报告 resampling strategy；Abstract 加 "approximately" 或 CI。

**SF-3** [warning] **DIF Audit Protocol (Section 5.4) 过于简略**
- 仅 3 句话，对于声称 "LLM benchmarks need psychometric monitoring" 的论文，practical recommendations 深度不足。ρ ≈ 0.997 说明 aggregate ranking 不受影响，但 25% 模型 rank shift ≥ 100——何时需要 action vs. 只需 annotation 未讨论。
- R2 提出 (W1)，R1 AGREE。

**SF-4** [warning] **ΔFPR = 0.000 的 robustness check 不足**
- 注入率固定 20%，仅 5 个 subjects 验证，未讨论 Type II error（cross-subject matching 是否过于保守降低 TPR）。
- R2 提出 (W2)，R1 AGREE（与 MF-1 互补）。

**SF-5** [warning] **K = 5 分层敏感性分析未在主文呈现**
- Appendix K = 20 θ-conditioning FPR = 0.312 ≈ K = 5，但此关键信息埋在附录。应在主文对比 K = 5/10/20。
- R1 提出 (W1)，R2 AGREE。

**SF-6** [warning] **Yen's Q3 局部依赖对 C% 的 inflation 未量化**
- 11.3% within-subject pairs 超 |Q3| > 0.20 是 non-trivial。应通过 simulation 估计此水平 LD 对 Type I error rate 的影响。
- R1 提出 (W2)，R2 AGREE。

**SF-7** [warning] **DIF 术语对 EMNLP 读者不够友好**
- ETS Category A/B/C、α_MH、Δ_MH 对 NLP 社区陌生。缺少 running example。
- R2 提出 (W3)，R1 AGREE。

**SF-8** [warning] **与 Growing Pains 的区分缺乏实证验证**
- 论文仅一句 "our work is complementary"，未验证 DIF-flagged items 与 FPC anchor items 的 overlap（Jaccard/enrichment）。
- R2 提出 (W4)，R1 AGREE。

**SF-9** [warning] **引用问题**
- 8 个 orphan bib entries（chen2025irtnet, choi2026llmjudgeirt, li2025atlas, liang2023helm, mialon2023gaia, ott2022benchmark, wang2024mmlupro, zhang2024gsm1k）
- holland1988mh year mismatch (bib=1988 vs possible 1986)
- tao2026selfcritique, liu2026bhi 的 first author mismatch
- R2 提出 (W5)，R1 AGREE。

**SF-10** [warning] **Claim 2 (ground truth meta-critique) 逻辑可逆**
- DIF ⊥ web-overlap 有两种等价解读：(A) web-overlap 是 wrong ground truth，或 (B) DIF 不检测 contamination。Semi-synthetic 部分化解了 B（ΔTPR > 0），但生态效度受限（见 MF-1）。
- R1 提出 (W6)，R2 REBUT with nuance：semi-synthetic 证明 DIF 可以检测 contamination，是 interpretive gap 而非 methodological flaw。

**SF-11** [warning] **Power analysis 假设不透明**
- Section 4.1 声称 N ≥ 80 达到 TPR 0.68–0.80，但未报告 IRT 参数、effect size、simulation procedure。
- R1 提出 (W5)，R2 AGREE。

---

## Reviewer Consensus

以下问题两位 Reviewer 均认为需要改进（标注 [AGREE]）：

1. **Claim 4 / GSM1K N = 33 不足以支撑独立 contribution** → 降级为 exploratory observation
2. **Semi-synthetic framework 生态效度有限** → 需 multi-subject injection 或限定 scope
3. **"Psychometric Instability" framing 过强** → 需软化 causal claims + 补充 within-year 分析
4. **DIF Audit Protocol 过于简略** → 需扩展 actionable recommendations
5. **K = 5 sensitivity 应在主文呈现**
6. **Q3 局部依赖影响应量化**
7. **DIF 术语需要 running example**
8. **Growing Pains overlap 分析应补充**
9. **引用需清理和修正**

---

## Reviewer Divergence

以下问题存在分歧（已通过交叉回应解决或记录）：

1. **Novelty 严重程度**：R2 列为 critical，R1 REBUT → 降级 Should-Fix。R1 论点：论文已 self-position 为 empirical contribution，EMNLP 接受此类工作，但需补充 LLM-specific challenges 讨论。R2 交叉回应后 Excitement 上调至 3.5/5，部分接受。

2. **Bootstrap CI 严重程度**：R1 列为 critical，R2 REBUT → 降级 Should-Fix。R2 论点：CI 完全在 "roughly one-third" 范围内，qualitative conclusion 不变。

3. **Within-family matched C% ≈ 28.3% 的解读**：R1 认为可能是 K = 5 下的 floor effect；R2 REBUT——K = 20 θ-conditioning 结果（FPR = 0.312）与 K = 5 无差别，反驳 floor effect 假说。分歧保留但非关键。

4. **Claim 2 逻辑可逆性**：R1 认为是 Should-Fix；R2 认为 semi-synthetic 已部分化解，是 interpretive gap 而非 methodological flaw。R2 的 Soundness 4 → 3.5 的调整已考虑此因素。

---

## Questions（需要作者回应）

1. Bootstrap resampling 的 unit 是什么（model-level vs. item-level）？不同策略下 CI 是否有实质变化？
2. 如果同时对 k 个 subjects 注入 contamination，cross-subject matching 的 ΔFPR 如何随 k 变化？
3. 论文如何回应 "31% DIF 中有多少比例是 legitimate capability evolution 而非 measurement instability" 的问题？是否有定量 decomposition 的可能性？
4. Yen's Q3 的 11.3% within-subject violation rate 对 C% 的 expected inflation 量级是多少？
5. DIF-flagged items 与 Growing Pains 的 FPC anchor items 的 overlap 是多少？

---

## Missing References

两位 Reviewer 共同确认以下遗漏（应在 Related Work 中引用或讨论）：

1. **"Lifting the Benchmark Iceberg with Item-Response Theory"** (2025, OpenReview) — IRT 揭示 benchmark 实现细节对排名的影响，与本文高度相关
2. **"Benchmark2: Systematic Evaluation of LLM Benchmarks"** (2025, arXiv:2601.03986) — 系统评估 benchmark 质量的框架
3. **ATLAS / CAT-based LLM evaluation** (Li et al. 2025, 已在 bib 但未引用) — Computerized Adaptive Testing 与 item-level diagnosis 相关
4. **MedIRT / "Measuring Competency, Not Performance"** (2025, arXiv:2509.24186) — 将 IRT 应用于医学 benchmark

此外，8 个 orphan bib entries 应清理。

---

## External Review (Stanford Agentic Reviewer)

外部审稿：未配置。

---

## Visual Layout Check（非评分项）

视觉审查已完成（2 warnings, 7 info）：

**Warnings:**
- **Pages 3–5**：连续 3 页无 figure/table（Figure 1 在 p2，下一个 Table 1 在 p6，间隔 4 页），p4-5 有 display equations 和 Proposition 1 缓解了单调感，但快速翻阅时视觉节奏偏平
- **Pages 1–8**：主正文 8 页仅 4 个 display items（2 fig + 2 table），对实证论文偏低（典型 5–8 个）。建议将 Figure 4（downstream ranking impact）或 Table 3（structural controls）提升到主正文

**Info:**
- 首页无视觉锚点，Figure 1 在 p2 顶部，可接受
- Figure 2 axis labels 字号偏小（约 60–65% 正文字号），建议 ≥ 70%
- 末页 40% 空白可优化
- Figure 4 散点图部分点重叠密集区辨识度降低
- Tables 使用 booktabs，格式规范
- 公式和 Proposition 排版正确
- References 格式统一，无 [?] 未解析引用

---

## Overall Assessment

**Overall Score: 3.5 / 5 — Borderline Conference**

| 维度 | R1 | R2 | 综合 |
|------|-----|-----|------|
| Soundness | 3.5 | 3.5 | 3.5 |
| Excitement | 3.5 | 3.5 | 3.5 |
| Reproducibility | 4 | 4 | 4 |
| Clarity | 4 | 4 | 4 |

**置信度**: 4/5（两位 Reviewer 均仔细通读全文及附录，对 DIF 方法论和 LLM evaluation 文献熟悉）

**判断依据**：
- 这是一篇 well-executed 的大规模实证研究，核心发现（31% MMLU items 跨代不稳定）对 benchmark 使用者有直接价值
- 6 个 benchmark 的交叉验证和 5 种 cohort design 的 confound 控制展示了扎实的方法论
- 但存在 3 个 critical 问题：(1) semi-synthetic 框架生态效度不足（single-subject only），(2) 因果 framing 过强（instability vs. capability evolution 不可区分），(3) Claim 4 证据不足
- 方法论新颖性有限——核心是 off-the-shelf psychometric method 的高质量应用，contributions 偏 descriptive
- 如果 MF-1 至 MF-3 得到有效 address（特别是 MF-2 的 framing 软化和 MF-3 的 Claim 4 降级），论文有望上升到 4.0（Conference）水平
- 当前定位：strong Findings / borderline Conference

**EMNLP 2026 评分映射**：3.5 = Borderline Conference（可被主会议接收的边界线）

**如果作者只能修复 3 个问题，优先级为**：
1. 软化 causal framing + 标题（MF-2）— 成本低、影响大
2. 补充 multi-subject injection 实验（MF-1）— 加强核心 validation
3. 降级 Claim 4 为 observation（MF-3）— 避免 overclaiming

---

## Detailed Comments（逐节）

### Title &amp; Abstract
- 标题 "Psychometric Instability" 过强，建议改为 "Differential Item Functioning" 或 "Measurement Drift"
- Abstract 数字过度密集（ρ≈0.997, z=-8σ to -49σ, ρ=-0.597 等），建议精简至 3-4 个核心数字
- Abstract 中 "31%" 应加 "approximately" 或报告 CI

### Section 1 (Introduction)
- Claim 4 应降级或移除，收紧为 3 个 contributions
- "Our contributions are empirical and methodological, not algorithmic" (L19) 的 self-positioning 是合适的，但后续 Method 部分未充分展开 LLM-specific challenges

### Section 2 (Related Work)
- DIF 方法论引用已较全面（Holland & Thayer 1988, Clauser 1998, Nandakumar 1993 等）
- 与 Growing Pains 的区分过于简短——需补充 overlap 分析
- 缺失 4 篇 2025-2026 重要相关工作（见 Missing References）

### Section 3 (Method)
- MH-DIF 描述准确但对 NLP 读者不够友好——需 running example
- Proposition 1 表述应更谨慎——明确其仅适用于 single-subject injection
- Cross-subject matching 的 prior art 定位（Nandakumar 1993, Donoghue 1993）已在文中，但应更明确是 application 而非 invention

### Section 4 (Experiments)
- Section 4.1 power analysis 缺细节
- Section 4.2 应增加 K sensitivity 和 bootstrap resampling unit 信息
- Section 4.5 semi-synthetic 需讨论 multi-subject 场景
- Section 4.6 downstream impact 的 model-specific rank shifts 值得从附录提升到正文
- Section 4.7 cross-benchmark 6-month window 结果（C% = 34.35%）仅 one-liner，应展开

### Section 5 (Discussion)
- Section 5.1: 应明确承认 "instability" 与 "capability evolution" 不可分
- Section 5.2: 应承认 Claim 2 的双向解读可能性
- Section 5.3: GSM1K 应标注 preliminary/exploratory
- Section 5.4: DIF Audit Protocol 需大幅扩展

### Limitations
- 覆盖全面（方法论、能力混淆、样本量、因果归因、benchmark 范围、temporal 定义）
- 略长（6 段），可适当精简

### References
- 8 orphan entries 待清理
- holland1988mh year 待核实
- 部分 first author mismatch 待修正

### Formatting
- 正文 8 页，合规
- Double-blind 匿名性合规
- ACL template 合规
- 主正文 display items 偏少（4 个），建议从附录提升 1-2 个
---

## ⚠️ 处理协议(强制,逐条执行,禁止简略)

**收到上面的审稿报告后必须按以下顺序处理**:

1. **逐条编号通读**: 把报告里所有问题、修改建议一项一项列出来(R1, R2, R3...),
   即使有的看起来重复或次要也要单独保留 — 不允许漏任何一条。

2. **每条给出详细修复方案**:
   - 补实验: 写明实验配置(baseline / 超参 / seed / dataset / metric) + 预估 GPU 时长
   - 改论文: 写明改哪一节、改写思路、新引用是否需要补
   - 改 framing / 措辞: 写明原文 + 改写后版本
   - 写 rebuttal: 写明论点和支撑证据
   - 拒绝: 写明拒绝理由(reviewer 误读 / 超出 scope / 已有反驳证据)

3. **必须调 `post_to_reviewer` 让 Research Reviewer 补充意见**:
   把完整审稿报告 + 你的逐条初步方案打包发给 Reviewer, 让 ta 独立审视有没有
   遗漏、方案可行性、技术细节是否合理。**这一步不可省** — Reviewer 是你的
   独立审查员, 他对每条审稿意见的判断和你互补。

4. **收到 Reviewer 补充后综合定稿**: 把 reviewer 反馈合并进你的 Action Matrix,
   再安排 Main Agent 执行(补实验 / 改论文)或向用户确认 venue 变更。

5. **同步 Issue Tracker**: Action Matrix 定稿后, 用 `read_local_file` 读
   `projects/{project}/artifacts/review_issues.yaml`, 把本轮处理的 issue 标
   `in_progress`、Reviewer 已确认修复的标 `resolved`, 调 `update_review_issues`
   写回。这是跨轮次的修复追踪 — Continue 模式审稿要靠这个表识别复发问题。

**禁止**:
- 跳过第 3 步直接派 Main Agent
- 把多条审稿意见合并成一条 action
- 对低 priority 意见用"暂不处理"一笔带过而不给出明确理由
- 整体性"已知悉"回复

```text
1	---
2	name: review-report-action
3	description: '当 Research Director 收到 Review 阶段回传的审稿报告时,立即使用本 skill。先做 Holistic Pass(识别 SYSTEMIC_ISSUE),再做 Per-Comment Action Matrix。禁止"已知悉"整体回复。'
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	---
8	
9	# Review Report Action Matrix
10	
11	## 硬约束
12	收到审稿报告后**禁止**整体性 "已知悉" 回复。必须**先做 Holistic Pass,再做 Per-Comment Action Matrix**。
13	
14	## Step 1: Holistic Pass（整体审视，action matrix 之前必做）
15	
16	审稿意见不是独立的,经常多条指向同一个系统性问题。切碎成多条独立 action 会漏掉根因。
17	
18	1. **聚类**：把所有审稿意见按主题聚类（method design / experiment design / claim strength / writing clarity / novelty / related work）
19	2. **识别系统性问题**：任何一个 cluster 含 ≥ 3 条意见 → 标记为 `[SYSTEMIC_ISSUE]`,不再切碎成独立 action,作为单独根因处理
20	3. **矩阵回溯**：如果 SYSTEMIC_ISSUE 指向 core method design → 立即触发 `experiment-matrix` skill 回顾:
21	   - 现有实验矩阵在新的 method 理解下还有几格有效？
22	   - 哪些之前的 positive 实验可能因此失效（method 基础变了）？
23	   - 哪些格子要重新跑？
24	4. **全局影响评估**：SYSTEMIC_ISSUE 是否影响论文 core claim？如果是 → 必须子调 `red-section` 做 framing 评估,然后再动 action matrix
25	
26	**Holistic Pass 输出写入 ROADMAP.md 的"审稿回溯·系统性问题"section**,先于 action matrix。
27	
28	## Step 2: Action Matrix 格式
29	
30	| 审稿意见 ID | 内容摘要 | 类别 | 动作 | 负责人 | ETA |
31	|-----------|--------|------|------|-------|-----|
32	| R1.1 | 缺某 ablation | 补实验 | 让 Agent 铺 exp_XXX | Agent | 2d |
33	| R1.2 | Claim 过强 | 改 paper | 调 red-section 重写 intro | Agent | 1d |
34	| R1.3 | 某 method 解释不清 | 写 rebuttal | Director 写 rebuttal 论点 | Director | 1d |
35	| R2.1 | Core claim 不成立 | framing 调整 | 子调 red-section + 改矩阵 | Agent + Director | 5d |
36	
37	## 类别
38	- **补实验**：必须让 Agent 铺实验（可与 rebuttal 并行）
39	- **改 paper**：让 Agent 调 red-section 后改章节
40	- **写 rebuttal**：Director 主笔
41	- **拒绝**：有理由的拒绝（写入 action matrix 的"拒绝理由"字段）
42	
43	## 产出
44	- action matrix 写入 ROADMAP.md 的"审稿回溯"section
45	- 决策日志同步（`log_decision`）
46	- 补实验条目立即调度（不等 rebuttal 写完）
47	- 如果有 "framing 调整" 类别 → 子调 red-section skill
48	
49	## Step 3: 同步 Issue Tracker（必做）
50	
51	action matrix 定稿后,同步更新跨轮次 Issue Tracker `artifacts/review_issues.yaml`:
52	
53	1. 用 `read_local_file("projects/{project}/artifacts/review_issues.yaml")` 读当前 tracker（不存在则视为空）
54	2. 对 action matrix 中每条:
55	   - 找到对应的 issue ID（同一 cluster / 相似 summary）
56	   - 把 `status` 从 `open` 翻到 `in_progress`
57	   - 在 `action_items` 里追加本轮 action（含 round / action / assigned_to / status）
58	3. 对上轮标 `in_progress` 且本轮 Reviewer 已确认修复（`[RESOLVED]`）的:
59	   - `status` 改为 `resolved`,记 `resolution_round` 和 `resolution`
60	4. 调 `update_review_issues(project, issues_yaml=完整 YAML)` 写回
61	
62	## 提交下一轮审稿（如适用）
63	
64	调 `submit_paper_to_review` 时根据情景设 `recommended_review_mode`:
65	
66	- **`continue`**：刚迭代修了上轮问题,需要 Reviewer 带着上轮记忆验证修复效果
67	- **`fresh`**：做了重大 reframing / 距上次审稿 >48h / 论文结构大改 → 想要不受干扰的客观评估
68	
69	同时在 `mode_reason` 写一句推荐理由。人类仍可在审批卡片上覆盖。
70	
```

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now let me do the systematic processing:

## Step 1: Holistic Pass

Let me cluster all issues by theme:

### Cluster 1: Causal/Interpretive Framing (5 items → SYSTEMIC_ISSUE)
- MF-2: "Psychometric Instability" vs "Expected Capability Evolution"
- SF-10: Claim 2 logic reversibility
- TA-1: Title too strong
- Section 5.1: Should acknowledge "instability" vs "capability evolution" indistinguishable


- Q3: Proportion of legitimate capability evolution

### Cluster 2: Semi-synthetic Framework Validity (4 items → SYSTEMIC_ISSUE)
- MF-1: Single-subject injection only
- SF-4: ΔFPR=0.000 robustness check insufficient
- SF-10: Claim 2 logic reversibility (overlaps with cluster 1)
- Q2: Multi-subject injection ΔFPR

### Cluster 3: Claim Strength / Overclaiming (4 items → SYSTEMIC_ISSUE)
- MF-3: Claim 4 evidence insufficient
- SF-3: Audit Protocol too brief
- TA-2: Abstract number density
- TA-3: "31%" should add CI

### Cluster 4: Method Presentation / Reader Accessibility (3 items → SYSTEMIC_ISSUE)
- SF-1: Method novelty argument insufficient
- SF-7: DIF terminology unfriendly
- SF-5: K=5 sensitivity not in main text

### Cluster 5: Technical Gaps (4 items)
- SF-2: Bootstrap CI incomplete
- SF-6: Q3 local dependence inflation
- SF-8: Growing Pains overlap
- SF-11: Power analysis assumptions

### Cluster 6: Citations/References (2 items)
- SF-9: Citation issues
- Missing References (4 papers)

### Cluster 7: Visual/Layout (2 items)
- V-1: Pages 3-5 no figures
- V-2: Only 4 display items

Now I'm identifying the systemic patterns across these clusters—starting with the causal inference issues that cut across multiple findings.

The core problem is that the paper frames MH-DIF as definitively detecting contamination, but the method can't actually distinguish between contamination and genuine capability evolution. Reviewers flagged this repeatedly, and the language throughout doesn't adequately hedge this limitation. There's also a scope constraint: the validation only tests single-subject injection, which doesn't fully support the broader claims about multi-subject scenarios. And several claims are simply overstated relative to what the evidence actually shows.

The good news is these aren't fundamental method failures—they're framing and scope issues that can be fixed through clearer writing and one targeted experiment adding multi-subject injection to the validation.

Now I'm mapping out what needs to change for each issue, starting with the multi-subject injection experiment.

I need to modify the existing injection experiment to test simultaneous contamination across multiple subjects, measuring how the false positive rate changes as I increase the number of contaminated subjects. This will either show that the method breaks down with multiple subjects (requiring a caveat) or holds up (strengthening the claim). The analysis is CPU-only and should take 4-6 hours.

For the framing, I'm shifting away from "instability" language toward "differential functioning" or "measurement drift," which is more precise about what's actually being measured. I'll add an explicit section distinguishing between item-level instability and broader capability evolution, and check whether the dataset has enough 2024 models to do a within-year comparison that would help isolate contamination from natural drift.

The feasibility check on the temporal comparison is tricky since METABENCH only goes through mid-2024. I'm downgrading Claim 4 about DIF as a diagnostic tool—removing it from the main contributions and repositioning the GSM1K findings as exploratory observations in the discussion instead. That reduces the paper to three core claims. I'm also adding a dedicated section on LLM-specific challenges like local dependence and non-normal ability distributions that arise when treating models as test-takers. The bootstrap reporting needs clarification on the resampling unit and a minor language adjustment in the abstract. The DIF audit protocol is already expanded in an earlier draft.

The expanded protocol still feels incomplete to reviewers, so I need to add concrete guidance on when to take action versus when to annotate findings, specify decision thresholds, and include a worked example. The ΔFPR robustness analysis connects to the multi-subject injection work already planned. I should also report performance across different injection rates. Moving the K sensitivity comparison from the appendix to the main text will help readers understand that choice. For the local dependence question, I need to run a simulation to estimate how much Type I error inflates when Q3 exceeds 0.20—that's a new experiment requiring a few hours of computation.

For the DIF section, I'll add an illustrative example to make the methodology concrete. The Growing Pains overlap analysis requires checking whether that dataset is accessible; if not, I'll discuss the conceptual relationship instead. I should clean up the bibliography by removing orphaned entries and fixing author/year inconsistencies. The reversibility concern in Claim 2 is partially addressed by the semi-synthetic results showing ΔTPR>0, but I'll make that connection explicit. Finally, I need to document the power analysis more thoroughly by reporting the IRT parameters, effect sizes, and simulation details used.

For the missing references, I'm adding four papers to strengthen the literature foundation. I should also consider promoting one or two key figures from the appendix to the main text for better visual impact.

Given the May 25 deadline, I need to work strategically. Today I'll tackle the title framing, downgrade Claim 4, and handle citation cleanup plus the missing references—all Paper Agent tasks. Tomorrow I'll focus on the multi-subject injection experiment and the Q3 simulation, then move through the writing improvements across multiple sections. By day three I should be ready for the Research Reviewer's independent assessment.
```

```json
  "content": "[Reviewer 回复]:\n## 独立审查 — Round 5 审稿处理方案评估\n\n---\n\n### Q1: 3 个 SYSTEMIC 判断是否正确？\n\n**SYSTEMIC_1 (因果 framing)**: ✅ 判断正确。纯语言/framing 问题，不影响方法或实验有效性。MH-DIF 确实无法区分 contamination vs capability evolution，当前标题暗示了不该暗示的因果方向。\n\n**SYSTEMIC_2 (Semi-synthetic scope)**: ✅ 判断正确，但需补充一个细节。当前论文表述 \"ΔFPR=0.000\" 可能让 reviewer 理解为无条件保证。**修复不仅需要 plan_034 实验，还需在 Proposition 1 表述中显式加 scope 限定词**（\"under single-subject injection\"）。如果只跑实验不改表述，same problem persists。\n\n**SYSTEMIC_3 (Overclaiming)**: ✅ 判断正确。paper_framing.md 在 D013 后已明确 GSM1K N=33 evidence 不足。正式降级是自然的。\n\n**\"不触及 core method，不需重跑已有实验\"**: 基本同意。plan_034 是 scope extension 而非 invalidation——现有 k=1 的 ΔFPR=0 结论仍然成立，plan_034 测试的是当 k>1 时这个性质是否 gracefully degrade。实验矩阵格子有效。\n\n---\n\n### Q2: plan_034 multi-subject injection 设计\n\nk∈{2,3,5,10} 的范围 OK，但有 4 个建议：\n\n1. **[severity: warning] Subject 选择协议缺失**——哪 k 个 subject 被注入？高 DIF subjects vs 低 DIF subjects vs 随机选择会产生不同结果。建议：**每个 k 做 ≥5 次随机抽取，报告 ΔFPR 均值±std**。\n\n2. **加 k=1 作为 baseline**——与 plan_016 结果交叉验证，确认代码一致性。0 额外成本。\n\n3. **考虑加 k=20**——20/57≈35%，给出更完整的退化曲线。如果 k=10 时 ΔFPR 仍≈0，k=20 给出更强的结论；如果 k=10 时已开始上升，k=20 确认趋势。\n\n4. **同时追踪 ΔTPR**——multi-subject contamination 可能稀释检测功效（clean subjects 的匹配变量噪声上升）。如果 ΔFPR 保持 0 但 ΔTPR 大幅下降，论文需要 note。\n\n**预测**：57 subjects 中注入 k=10 仍有 47 个 clean subjects 贡献匹配变量。ΔFPR 大概率在 k≤5 时保持 ≈0，k=10 可能出现小幅上升（~1-3pp）。这个结果对论文是最佳情况——可以写 \"cross-subject matching is robust to moderate multi-domain contamination (k≤5), with graceful degradation at k=10\"。\n\n---\n\n### Q3: 标题——\"Measurement Drift\" vs \"Differential Item Functioning\"\n\n**推荐 \"Measurement Drift\"**，理由：\n\n1. ## 独立审查 — Round 5 审稿处理方案评估
```

---

### Q1: 3 个 SYSTEMIC 判断是否正确？

**SYSTEMIC_1 (因果 framing)**: ✅ 判断正确。纯语言/framing 问题，不影响方法或实验有效性。MH-DIF 确实无法区分 contamination vs capability evolution，当前标题暗示了不该暗示的因果方向。

**SYSTEMIC_2 (Semi-synthetic scope)**: ✅ 判断正确，但需补充一个细节。当前论文表述 "ΔFPR=0.000" 可能让 reviewer 理解为无条件保证。**修复不仅需要 plan_034 实验，还需在 Proposition 1 表述中显式加 scope 限定词**（"under single-subject injection"）。如果只跑实验不改表述，same problem persists。

**SYSTEMIC_3 (Overclaiming)**: ✅ 判断正确。paper_framing.md 在 D013 后已明确 GSM1K N=33 evidence 不足。正式降级是自然的。

**"不触及 core method，不需重跑已有实验"**: 基本同意。plan_034 是 scope extension 而非 invalidation——现有 k=1 的 ΔFPR=0 结论仍然成立，plan_034 测试的是当 k>1 时这个性质是否 gracefully degrade。实验矩阵格子有效。

---

### Q2: plan_034 multi-subject injection 设计

k∈{2,3,5,10} 的范围 OK，但有 4 个建议：

1. **[severity: warning] Subject 选择协议缺失**——哪 k 个 subject 被注入？高 DIF subjects vs 低 DIF subjects vs 随机选择会产生不同结果。建议：**每个 k 做 ≥5 次随机抽取，报告 ΔFPR 均值±std**。

2. **加 k=1 作为 baseline**——与 plan_016 结果交叉验证，确认代码一致性。0 额外成本。

3. **考虑加 k=20**——20/57≈35%，给出更完整的退化曲线。如果 k=10 时 ΔFPR 仍≈0，k=20 给出更强的结论；如果 k=10 时已开始上升，k=20 确认趋势。

4. **同时追踪 ΔTPR**——multi-subject contamination 可能稀释检测功效（clean subjects 的匹配变量噪声上升）。如果 ΔFPR 保持 0 但 ΔTPR 大幅下降，论文需要 note。

**预测**：57 subjects 中注入 k=10 仍有 47 个 clean subjects 贡献匹配变量。ΔFPR 大概率在 k≤5 时保持 ≈0，k=10 可能出现小幅上升（~1-3pp）。这个结果对论文是最佳情况——可以写 "cross-subject matching is robust to moderate multi-domain contamination (k≤5), with graceful degradation at k=10"。

---

### Q3: 标题——"Measurement Drift" vs "Differential Item Functioning"

**推荐 "Measurement Drift"**，理由：

1. NLP audience 熟悉 "drift" 概念（concept drift, data drift），零学习成本
2. 不暗示因果方向——"drift" 是中性描述词
3. "Differential Item Functioning" 对非 psychometrician 是 jargon barrier
4. 标题是 marketing，不是技术定义——precision 留给 Abstract 和 Method

**具体建议**：标题用 "Temporal Measurement Drift"，Abstract 第一句引入 "Differential Item Functioning (DIF)"。例如：

> *Temporal Measurement Drift in LLM Benchmarks: A Large-Scale Psychometric Audit*

"Drift" 的一个微妙不精确：DIF 度量的是 cross-group 差异而非时间趋势。但在本文 context 下（temporal cohort 是 grouping variable），这种不精确是可接受的，且远好于 "Instability" 的因果暗示。

---

### Q4: Claim 4——删除 vs 合并

**推荐合并进 Claim 1**，不是直接删除。理由：

"Diagnostic lens" 是论文叙事的 keystone——"instability ≠ invalidity" 需要 "so what" 的回答。如果直接删除 Claim 4，Claim 1 的 "31% items show DIF" 悬在空中缺乏 actionable implication。

**3-contribution 结构**：
1. **Temporal DIF at scale + diagnostic implications**：31% DIF + robustness checks + downstream rank impact（plan_032: ρ=0.997 掩盖 89.5% 局部扰动 + cohort asymmetry z=4.35）+ item characterization（plan_023: difficulty×direction ρ=-0.597）+ audit protocol
2. **Ground truth meta-critique**：enrichment≈1.0 + ConStat comparison + empirical validation
3. **Semi-synthetic validation framework**：ΔTPR/ΔFPR + cross-subject matching + cross-benchmark portability

这样 plan_020 circularity ablation 和 plan_023 item characterization 成为 Claim 1 的 supporting evidence，GSM1K 去 Discussion，audit protocol 从独立贡献降为 Claim 1 的 practical implication。

---

### Q5: 4 天时间线可行性

**可行，但有 3 个高风险环节**：

1. **[最高风险] 页面预算冲突**——当前 10 页需压缩到 8 页（-2 页），但 action matrix 大量增加内容：SF-1（+1-2 段）、SF-3（+1 段 protocol 扩展）、SF-7（+running example）、SF-11（+power analysis）、V-1/V-2（+1-2 display items）。净增约 1.5-2 页内容。**总计需压缩 3.5-4 页**。MF-3（Claim 4 降级）省 ~0.5 页，appendix 移入省一些，但远不够。**建议 Director 明确列出被砍内容清单**，否则 5/23 integration 会陷入削足适履。

2. **[中风险] MF-2 pervasive language replacement**——"instability" 在 10 页论文中可能出现 30+ 次，mechanical find-replace 会导致行文僵硬。建议 front-load 到 5/21 下午启动，而非等到 5/22。Paper Agent 需要逐处审读 context，不能批量替换。

3. **[中风险] plan_034 结果解读**——如果 ΔFPR 在 k=2 就上升（概率低但非零），Proposition 1 caveat 变得复杂，需要重写 semi-synthetic section 而非仅加一句 scope 限定。

**建议调整**：
- MF-2 今天下午启动（不等到明天）
- plan_034/035 今晚启动（CPU, 可 overnight）
- 5/22 同时处理 SF items + 等实验结果
- **5/21 今天就确定页面砍削清单**

---

### Q6: Growing Pains——conceptual discussion 够不够？

**够。** 

Reviewer 问的是 "awareness of related work"，不是 "head-to-head comparison"。Growing Pains 是 45 篇 related work 中的一篇，不是 direct competitor。

建议写法：
1. 阐述互补性（anchor-item evaluation vs DIF diagnostic）
2. 指出 DIF 可以识别哪些 anchor items 正在退化（具体 bridge）
3. "Empirical comparison is future work pending public availability of anchor item lists"

**设置时间盒**：Growing Pains 数据搜索限 2 小时。找到就做 Jaccard，找不到就用上述 conceptual discussion。不值得超过 2 小时。

---

### Q7: 遗漏或明显问题

**[severity: warning] 3 个遗漏项**：

1. **Cross-benchmark bootstrap N=10 不可用**——我之前的审查已 flag：只有 MMLU 有 N=1000 bootstrap，其余 5 个 benchmark 的 CI 基于 N=10（min/max 伪装成 percentile CI）。如果 SF-2 要在论文中报告 cross-benchmark CI，**必须用 N≥500 重跑 ARC/HellaSwag/WinoGrande/TruthfulQA/GSM8K**。这不在 action matrix 中。建议：要么不在主文报 cross-benchmark CI（只报 MMLU），要么加入 overnight CPU task。

2. **plan_033 多重比较校正**——5 个 "excess DIF" subjects（plan_033 report）未做 BH 校正。professional_medicine (p=0.040) 在 57 次检验下几乎必然不显著。如果论文引用这个数字，需要校正后结果。不在 action matrix 中。

3. **plan_031 "DIF saturates in 6 months" 表述**——只有 2 个时间点（0.5yr 和 1.0yr），不能建立 saturation curve。如果论文使用 "saturates" 或类似表述，需要 hedge。MF-2 的 general framing 软化可能覆盖，但建议显式检查。

**[severity: info] 1 个 structural observation**：

Action matrix 没有 "拒绝项"（所有意见均接受）。这本身是正确判断——Round 5 的 comments 都有合理性。但如果 5/25 时间不够，需要有 triage 准备：SF-6 (Q3 simulation) 和 SF-8 (Growing Pains) 是最低 ROI 的两项，可以被 time-boxed 或推迟到 camera-ready。

```json
{
  "project": "irt_equated_bench_regen",
  "text": "**紧急任务：Round 5 审稿修复实验 (DDL 5/23 出结果)**\n\n## 任务 1: plan_034 Multi-Subject Simultaneous Injection (优先级最高)\n\n审稿 MF-1 指出：当前 semi-synthetic framework 仅验证 single-subject injection，Proposition 1 (ΔFPR=0) 在 multi-subject contamination 下可能不成立。\n\n**实验设计**：\n- 基于 plan_011 的 semi-synthetic injection 框架（已有代码）\n- 对 k 个 subjects 同时注入 contamination（incorrect→correct flipping, p=0.5）\n- k ∈ {1, 2, 3, 5, 10, 20}（1 是 baseline 与 plan_016 交叉验证）\n- **每个 k 做 5 次随机 subject 抽取**，报告 ΔFPR 均值±std\n- 注入率也做 matrix: p ∈ {0.1, 0.3, 0.5}（至少 p=0.5 做完整 k sweep）\n- **同时追踪 ΔTPR**（检测功效是否随 k 增大而降低）\n- Cross-subject matching variable = 所有未注入 subjects 的总分\n\n**数据**：METABENCH MMLU response matrix（已有）\n**输出**：\n- ΔFPR(k) 和 ΔTPR(k) 曲线（k=1..20）\n- 在哪个 k ΔFPR 开始显著偏离 0？\n- 结论：cross-subject matching 对 multi-domain contamination 的容忍度\n\n**注册到 registry**: plan_034_multi_subject_injection\n**预估**: CPU only, 4-6 小时\n\n## 任务 2: plan_035 Q3 Local Dependence Inflation Simulation (次优先)\n\n审稿 SF-6 指出：11.3% within-subject item pairs 超 |Q3|>0.20 的局部依赖，对 MH-DIF Type I error rate 影响未量化。\n\n**实验设计**：\n- Monte Carlo simulation (10,000 runs)\n- 生成 response matrix with calibrated local dependence (11.3% pairs |Q3|>0.20)\n- 在无真实 DIF 条件下运行 MH-DIF\n- 测量 actual Type I error rate vs nominal 0.05\n- 对比：independent items (Q3≈0) vs observed LD level (11.3%)\n\n如果时间紧张可简化：直接用 METABENCH 数据做 within-subject permutation null（randomize temporal labels），在有 LD 的真实数据上建立经验 null distribution。\n\n**注册到 registry**: plan_035_q3_inflation_sim\n**预估**: CPU only, 2-4 小时\n\n## 任务 3: Growing Pains 数据查找 (2h time-box)\n\n审稿 SF-8 要求验证 DIF flags 与 Growing Pains FPC anchor items 的 overlap。\n- WebSearch \"Growing Pains LLM benchmark\" 找论文，查是否公开 anchor item 列表\n- 有 → 计算 Jaccard/enrichment\n- 无 → 直接报告\"数据不公开，无法做 empirical comparison\"\n- **限时 2 小时**，不值得更多时间\n\n三个任务按优先级执行。plan_034 最重要，plan_035 次之，Growing Pains 最后。所有结果必须注册到 registry。"
}
```
